# Review: Section 5

## Section

Section 5: Risk-neutral distribution (`PLAN.md` steps 5.1 and 5.2, with instruction 05, which adds step 5.0 and 4 corrections to the Section 4 surface).

**Stopped at step 5.1 under rule 4.** Step 5.0 is complete and its tests pass. Step 5.1 is built and committed, and `test_nonnegative_when_butterfly_passes` (kickoff 5.6: q ≥ 0 when the slice passed the butterfly check) fails on the fixture: on 45 of 74 accepted fixture slices the density is negative somewhere on the instruction 05 integration grid, although every one passes the butterfly check on the 801-point wide grid (S1). Beyond the fixture, the instruction 05 integral and mean checks (`int_tol` = 1e-3) fail on 23 of the 496 accepted slices of 2026-10-29, by up to 0.070 on the integral and 4.4% on the mean (D2). Choosing a fix is a design choice (O-36), so step 5.2 is not built. The failing test is committed as written.

## Steps completed

- 5.0  80877ab  step 5.0: SVI wing bound, wide butterfly grid, neighbour retry, slice metrics (with the part A decisions in `decisions/section_4_review.md`, O-33 marked decided and O-34 in `decisions/OPEN.md`)
- 5.1  9acf0df  step 5.1: density (q >= 0 test fails on the fixture, section stopped) (O-35 and O-36 in `decisions/OPEN.md`)
- 5.2  not built (stopped, see S1)

## Evidence

### S1. Why the section stopped

Every accepted fixture slice from `fit_expiries` (the step 5.0 fit, run per ticker on the fixture day), checked 3 ways: g on the 801-point wide grid (`min_g_801`, what the fit enforces), g on the 200,001-point integration grid over [−L, L] (`min_g_int`, with the k range where g < −1e-10 as `neg_from` to `neg_to`), and g on 600,001 points over [−3, 3] (`min_g_pm3`). `P_above_2F` and `P_below_halfF` are the O-35 (a) closed-form tail probabilities at 2F and F/2; `tail_min` and `tail_max` are the smallest and largest probability in the slice's `tail_table`. Only slices with a failure are listed.

Two separate failures on the fixture:

1. **Between grid points.** On 45 of 74 slices (all on the `refit` route) g is negative between 2 points of the 801-point grid, over a gap about 1 grid step wide (`neg_from` to `neg_to`), down to −7.6e-6 (AAPL 2027-01-15). The hard SLSQP constraint pushes g to 0 at the grid points, and the integration grid, 250 times finer, sees between them.
2. **Beyond ±L.** On `^SPX` 2026-11-06, SPY 2026-11-04 and 2026-11-05 (all `first` route) and AAPL 2026-11-06, g is negative outside the wide grid (down to −0.061 at k = 1.648 on `^SPX` 2026-11-06, where L = 0.547). The closed-form P(S_T > 2F) is then −4.6e-4, and the +20% row of the tail table is −7.9e-5.

On 2026-10-29 the second failure is larger. These are the 23 accepted slices of that day whose integral or mean misses `int_tol`, with their wings b(1 − ρ) and b(1 + ρ) and the smallest g inside ±L and inside ±3L:

A third cause shows on long dated slices (MU, NVDA, BAC 2027 expiries): g is at most −2e-6 in size, but L = 24√w(0) is sized from the ATM variance while the wing variance is far larger, so 0.1% to 0.24% of the mass lies beyond ±L.

**CI on `35d4f13`: failure** (run 41080293255, Linux), `3 failed, 4513 passed` in 7 min 57 s. Besides `test_nonnegative_when_butterfly_passes`, `test_integrates_to_one` and `test_mean_equals_forward` fail on Linux. There, the fixture `^SPX` 2026-11-05 slice fits differently from Windows (SLSQP takes a different path, as in session 4) and integrates to 1.0038 with a mean 0.53% above F. ## Runtime per step

- 5.0: `run_all.py --stage surface` over both days took 79 min 24 s (the session 4b run took 1,061 s). The wide-grid constraint and SLSQP's finite-difference Jacobians make each refit slower: the fixture `^SPX` day alone takes about 415 s.
- 5.1: the fixture-based SVI, density and tails tests together took 18 min 46 s, most of it fitting the fixture once (session fixture `fixture_fits`).

## Deviations from PLAN.md

- Step 5.2 is not built (rule 4 stop).
- `test_fixture_accepted_slices` now fits each ticker with `fit_expiries` (the neighbour retry needs every slice of the ticker), through a session fixture shared with `test_density.py`. Its assertion and the 0.75 share are unchanged.
- `test_surface_run_fixture` accepts the new SURFACE_DAILY statuses `no_bracket` and `delta_outside_quotes` (joined with `|`, O-34 (a)) and checks the new `route` column.
- `svi_fits.csv` gains `route`; `surface_daily.csv` gains `ssvi_rr25_30` after `rr25_30`.

## Not verified

Interpretations made to build step 5.0, for the reviewer to confirm:

1. The wing bound lives in config as `[svi] wing_max = 2.0`, beside the new `[svi] neighbour_retries = 3`; `[svi] grid_points` becomes 801 (instruction 05 correction 2), so the calendar grid has 801 points too; `[density] int_points = 200001` is added for the integration grid.
2. L in the wide grid uses w(0) of the parameters being judged, so inside the refit the constraint grid moves with the parameters, and the acceptance check uses the fit's own grid.
3. The neighbour retry runs on `poor_fit` and `butterfly_fail` slices only, uses the slices accepted in the first pass (ties in |ΔT| to the earlier expiry), and runs the constrained refit (g ≥ 0 on the wide grid) from the scaled neighbour parameters, with the same counting rule as O-32 (a). A retry that fails leaves the first-pass fit, status and route.
4. The ATM vol in SURFACE_DAILY uses the cumulative-maximum θ of SSVI (`theta_used`), so it equals the session 4b value; the 25-delta wings use each slice's own w at the matched delta.
5. A 25-delta root exactly at an end of the quoted range counts as inside it.
6. The full surface run was not repeated for a byte-identical check (79 minutes); `test_run_twice_byte_identical` passes on the fixture subset.

Not checked: whether the 2026-10-30 capture has landed; step 5.0 covers the fixture day and 2026-10-29 only, as instruction 05 asks.

To prepare PIT, the session reran `scripts/pull_data.py --stage prices`. `close.parquet` and `index.parquet` came back identical on every committed row with 2026-10-28 and 2026-10-29 added (`^GSPC` 7,720.39 and SPY 769.36 on 2026-10-29), `dividends.csv` was unchanged, and `sectors.parquet` adjusted closes moved by up to 6.9e-5. Since step 5.2 is not built, the refreshed files were **not** kept: `data/raw/` is as committed.

## Open questions

- **O-34** SURFACE_DAILY status when more than one thing fails. (a) Each failure blanks only its values, statuses joined with `|`; (b) one status by precedence, every metric blank when not `ok`. Implemented as (a).
- **O-35** How tail probabilities are computed. (a) The slice's closed-form distribution function; (b) a cumulative trapezoid integral on the integration grid. Implemented as (a) in `tail_table`.
- **O-36** The butterfly check does not make the density a valid distribution where Section 5 uses it (the stop). (a) Change the fit: a denser, wider butterfly check and a wing-based integration range, then rerun step 5.0; (b) keep the fit and gate the density with a status per slice, tail probabilities by O-35 (b). Not decided.

## Session 5b

Instruction `instructions/05b_section_5_density.md`: step 5b.1, then step 5.1 again and step 5.2.

### Section (session 5b)

**Stopped under rule 4 after step 5b.1, by the owner's choice.** Step 5b.1 is built, committed and its Section 4 rerun is done. Under it, every accepted slice's density integrates to 1 and has mean F within `int_tol` (largest errors 2.5e-9 and 3.6e-4 over 567 slices; 2 `density_fail` slices), and g ≥ −1e-10 on the 200,001-point sinh grid on every slice. `test_nonnegative_when_butterfly_passes` still fails, on a different assertion from session 5: its g check now passes, but the density itself is below 0 on 14 of 74 accepted fixture slices, by −5.3e-12 to −2.3e-15, everywhere that g lies in [−1e-10, 0), which the butterfly tolerance of kickoff 5.4 allows (5b-S1). Instruction 05b says the test must pass as written. Asked in session, the owner chose to stop under rule 4 rather than read q ≥ 0 within the tolerance. That choice is O-37. Steps 5.1 and 5.2 are not rebuilt.

**Every `density_fail` slice.** Both are `first`-route fits whose total variance w is slightly negative far out in a wing, beyond the quotes: down to −3.3e-13 at k ≈ 3.68 on CSCO 2026-11-13 and −1.3e-6 near k ≈ 7.9 on SHOP 2026-11-20 (2,242 grid points). The density is then NaN there, so the integral is NaN and the slice is `density_fail` (instruction 05b part C). They passed the acceptance check because g stays finite where w < 0: instruction 05b's rule that a non-finite g (from w ≤ 0) fails only catches w = 0 exactly. The first-route fit is not held to the exact Section 5.4 constraints (only refits are, O-32 (a)), and the SHOP first fit's a + b s √(1 − ρ²) sits slightly below 0. Reported, not changed:

### Deviations from PLAN.md (session 5b)

- Steps 5.1 and 5.2 are not rebuilt (rule 4 stop).
- `svi_fits.csv` gains `cut_rounds` (the cutting-plane rounds of the kept fit), so 5b-D1 can count them.
- `density_check` returns `min_g` and `density_status` beside the instruction 05b columns.
- `test_analytic_vs_fd_measure` is renamed `test_analytic_vs_fd` now that it asserts the bound.

### Not verified (session 5b)

1. The cutting points accumulate over the rounds (round 2 adds 10 more to round 1's 10), are fixed values of k, and the constraint for a refit is "met" when SLSQP reports success or the iteration limit, every Section 5.4 bound and constraint holds exactly, and g ≥ −1e-10 on the 801 points plus the cuts.
2. A start whose refit does not meet its constraint gets no cutting rounds and does not count.
3. `tail_mass` is added to the integral only; the mean is the trapezoid sum alone, as part B describes.
4. `[svi] grid_points` (801) is the constraint grid size and `[density] int_points` (200,001) the acceptance grid size; no new keys for them.
5. A NaN integral counts as `density_fail`.
6. A non-finite g is the only w ≤ 0 test (instruction 05b step 5b.1). It does not catch w < 0 with g finite, which happens on 2 `first`-route slices (5b-D2); the `density_fail` status catches both.

### Open questions (session 5b)

- **O-37** Exact q ≥ 0 against the butterfly tolerance. (a) q ≥ 0 holds up to the butterfly tolerance: the test asserts g ≥ −1e-10 on the integration grid and q ≥ 0 wherever g ≥ 0. (b) The acceptance check requires g ≥ 0 exactly, so q ≥ 0 holds exactly, and step 5b.1 is rerun.

## Session 5c

Instruction `instructions/05c_section_5_finish.md`: step 5c.1, then step 5.1 again and step 5.2.

### Section (session 5c)

**Section 5 is complete.** Step 5c.1 accepts a fit on any route only with w ≥ 1e-12 on the 200,001-point acceptance grid and every Section 5.4 bound and constraint met exactly, and O-37 (a) restates the non-negativity test. Section 4 was rerun over 3 days: 2026-10-28, 2026-10-29 and 2026-10-30. The 2026-10-30 capture was in the `snapshots` release when the session started, so it is the third day, per the instruction 05c status 8 decision. Against session 5b, only CSCO 2026-11-13 and SHOP 2026-11-20 on 2026-10-29 changed: both are now `ok_refit` with positive w, and neither is `density_fail` any more. Step 5.1 checks 1,013 accepted slices: 1,011 are `ok` and 2 are `density_fail`, both long-dated MU on 2026-10-30. Their mean misses `int_tol` by exactly the mean mass beyond k = +10, which the mean leaves out (5c-D2). Step 5.2 writes the tail tables for the 1,011 and the 2 PIT rows (`^SPX` and SPY 2026-10-29). The full suite passes.

There are two findings, both reported and not changed. On the owner's choice when asked in session, the section was finished rather than stopped:

- **O-38.** The finite-difference bound of instruction 05b, measured on every accepted slice, is broken by 109 of 1,013. 97 of those have s at its floor, and the analytic density is not at fault (5c-D2).
- **O-39.** The density stage reads the SVI parameters from `svi_fits.csv`, which rounds them to 10 significant digits. That moves g by up to about 5e-10 (5c-D2).

### 5c-D2. Instruction 05b part E items 2, 4 and 5, every day

**Every accepted slice.** Computed with `density_check` on the 200,001-point sinh grid over [−10, 10], using the `svi_fits.csv` parameters as written (`%.10g`) and each slice's F. `fit_min_g` is the `min_g` column of `svi_fits.csv`: the fit's own value on the same grid, at full precision. Per ticker per day, the table gives:
- the smallest g;
- the largest and median |integral − 1|, |mean / F − 1| and `tail_mass`;
- the smallest density;
- the `density_fail` count.

**Every `density_fail` slice.** Both are long-dated MU refits on 2026-10-30 whose mean misses `int_tol`. The integral itself is within 1e-10. The mean is a trapezoid sum over [−10, 10] with nothing added for the mass beyond, as instruction 05b part B specifies. That missing mass, E[e^k; k > 10] = ∫ e^k p_k dk from 10 by `quad`, equals the mean error to 4 digits (1.086010e-3 against −1.086e-3, and 1.707023e-3 against −1.707e-3), although P(k > 10) is only 8.6e-9 and 1.4e-8. Both slices have no tail table and no PIT value.

### Runtime per step (session 5c)

- 5c.1: IV stage for 2026-10-30, 19 min 32 s; surface stage over 3 days, 173 min 21 s; the tests of the changed files, 27 min 28 s.
- 5.1: `run_all.py --stage density`, 3 min 0 s.
- 5.2: `run_all.py --stage tails`, 1 min 0 s.
- Full suite: 30 min 54 s.

### Deviations from PLAN.md (session 5c)

- `pit.csv` has 2 rows. Only the 2026-10-29 expiries have passed by the last close in the price files.
- `spx_smiles_2026-10-29.png` stays in the repository from session 5b. The stage draws the first and last day, and 2026-10-29 is neither now.

### Not verified (session 5c)

1. `_violation` = 0 is the exact test of every Section 5.4 bound and constraint on the first route, as on the refits (O-32 (a)). A first fit that fails it, or fails the w floor, goes to the refit route.
2. The w floor is checked on the 200,001-point acceptance grid only. Between grid points w is not checked.
3. PIT covers every captured expiry on or before the last close, and the last close is the earlier of the last dates of `close.parquet` and `index.parquet`.

### Open questions (session 5c)

- **O-38** The finite-difference bound on every accepted slice. (a) The bound is the fixture test only, and the all-slice measure is reported. (b) The bound holds on every accepted slice: either the FD step is scaled to the slice, or Section 4 rejects slices whose density cannot be resolved.
- **O-39** The SVI parameters in `svi_fits.csv` are rounded to `%.10g`. (a) Write them at full precision, so steps 5.1 and 5.2 judge the accepted parameters. (b) Keep `%.10g`; the fit's own `min_g` is the acceptance record.
